- **mandela**: Audit any eval, metric, experiment, or benchmark for leakage — does external ground-truth enter independently, or are the model, scorer, and designer just confirming a result no outside truth ever produced? Walks an 8-pattern leakage taxonomy and returns only the patterns that fire, each with an independence fix. Read-only.
